# Every distance-3 factory class with at most 40 injections

**393 distinct `(n, k, S_k gate)` classes**, every check rank, one verified witness circuit each: the 74 rows of the shipped `n <= 38` classification (`../../exhaustive_n38/catalog/classification_n38.json`, copied verbatim, a3 computed from their columns) followed by the 319 rows of `classification_n3940.json` (best-a3 witnesses).

No factory exists at n in {17..22, 25, 26}: those lengths host no unital triorthogonal class (see `../../exhaustive_n38/docs/THEORY_EXHAUSTIVENESS.md`), and none at n = 16 or 24.
